Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) improves interpretability in decision making by summarizing preferences into natural-language principles, but its single-pass explanations miss much of the nuance involved in complex decisions. We introduce Democratic ICAI, a novel approach that gathers multiple competing rationales through structured persona debate, offering a broader and more expressive account of the factors influencing each comparison. From these richer signals, we derive clearer and more comprehensive steering principles and use them to guide decision modeling through both LLM-based and decision-tree judges. Experiments on creative preference benchmarks, MuCE-Pref and LiTBench, across multiple creative task categories show that Democratic ICAI yields a more faithful preference structure. It improves average preference prediction across tasks relative to deliberative prompting and principle-based baselines, while producing constitutions that LLM annotators prefer.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文受互联网发展启发，提出了世界AI模型网络（AI-ModelNet）的概念、愿景与系统架构，旨在通过建立模型间通路实现异构模型的互联互通、能力共享与协同推理，解决当前大模型应用面临的高成本、部署复杂及异构模型协作困难等瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型面临训练成本高昂、部署复杂等挑战，且随着异构模型快速普及，模型间有效交互与协作成为亟待解决的关键瓶颈。

Method: 借鉴互联网发展思路，提出AI-ModelNet的概念与分层架构体系，通过建立模型间通路实现互联互通与协同推理，并构建原型系统验证可行性。

Result: 通过原型系统与多样化应用案例验证了所提框架的可行性。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet为轻量化、私有化、领域化模型间的协作提供了一种全新范式，未来研究方向值得进一步探索。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [77] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了人格提示（尤其是宜人性）对多智能体LLM团队任务表现的影响，发现效果取决于任务结构：在结构化编码任务中影响小，但在开放式协作和竞争性谈判任务中显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究表明人格提示会改变LLM的沟通风格（如低宜人性产生对抗性语言，高宜人性产生合作性语言），但沟通风格的变化是否影响客观任务结果尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过操纵前沿LLM的人格特质（宜人性高低），在三个任务领域（结构化编码、开放式研究协作、竞争性谈判）中测试多智能体团队表现。

Result: 人格效应关键取决于任务结构。在编码任务中，低宜人性引发较大的沟通风格变化，但对里程碑完成度影响甚微；在开放式协作和谈判中，同样的操纵显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 人格提示对多智能体系统性能的影响具有任务依赖性，为多智能体系统设计提供了重要启示，并指出了人格操纵的局限性。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [78] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段训练范式（WM-AMT、FE-SFT、FC-RL），使LLM智能体通过内部世界模型进行前瞻性规划，在搜索与数学推理任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM智能体在长程任务中缺乏"what-if"推理能力，无法像人类一样在行动前通过内部世界模型模拟未来结果，导致行为本质上是反应式的。

Method: 提出三阶段训练范式：(i) WM-AMT注入潜在预测能力；(ii) FE-SFT结构化该能力；(iii) FC-RL通过强化学习优化生成的模拟的校准度和实用性。训练单一自回归模型同时生成状态展开轨迹和计划条件成功估计（文本Q值）。

Result: 在搜索和数学推理任务上，该方法一致优于其他训练基线，证明了能力优先的训练流水线能实现有根基且校准的前瞻性。

Conclusion: 有效的内部世界建模需要"能力优先"的训练流水线，单纯的微调前瞻性轨迹只能导致对远见的表面模仿，而无法获得真正的预测能力根基。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [79] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ODYSSEY——一个基于层论（sheaf theory）和范畴论（category theory）的形式化框架，用于构建可验证、局部保真的基础模型，通过名为foundry的构建块组件实现知识覆盖、局部表示、限制映射、粘合规则、障碍策略、更新义务及人机视图，并引入Universal Foundry Learning (UFL)和Foundry SQL (FSQL)等配套机制，在多种具体foundry上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型缺乏形式化的局部保真性和可验证性保证，需要一种能够将异构知识源以结构化、可组合、可审计的方式组织起来的理论框架，确保模型构建过程中的语义一致性、推理透明性和人为可监督性。

Method: 采用范畴论中的层论和Kan扩展机制：左Kan扩展将局部工件滚动为候选foundry，右Kan扩展强制执行限制、粘合、障碍和论证条件；引入Foundry SQL作为类型化查询接口，TICKET作为外部模型准入认证机制。整个框架由一系列泛化foundry（证据/论证、操作决策、制度/金融、市场意义、科学挑战、研究计划、助手构建、评估套件等）实例化。

Result: 在多种具体foundry上完成全实现和测试，覆盖领域构建、工件重放、层诊断、接地Toulmin/局部LLM审查、残差障碍分类账、以及跨异构源的TICKET兼容因果声明提取等任务。

Conclusion: 同一套范畴论机制能够统一支持基础模型构建中的多类核心任务，为构建可验证、可审计的AI系统提供了形式化理论基础和实用工具链。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [80] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DysLexLens，一个低资源 LLM 框架，用于通过在线论坛讨论分析失读症学习者的 AI 使用体验。该框架包含词典驱动过滤、知识图谱问答推理、定量评估和定性验证等模块，在 Reddit 数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失读症学习者越来越多地使用 AI 工具，但他们使用这些工具的真实体验尚未得到充分研究，缺乏专门分析其在线讨论的低资源框架。

Method: DysLexLens 是一个端到端、证据可追溯的架构：(1) 词典驱动的过滤方法构建聚焦的 Reddit 语料库；(2) LLM 辅助语义分析与知识图谱问答推理结合；(3) RAGAS 和查询鲁棒性等定量评估指标；(4) 结构化定性验证指南评估幻觉和证据对齐。

Result: 在失读症相关的 Reddit 论坛数据和 30 个问题上验证了 DysLexLens 的有效性，展示了其对其他低资源论坛数据上下文的潜在泛化能力。

Conclusion: DysLexLens 能够有效分析失读症学习者的 AI 使用体验，具有良好的可泛化性和可复现性，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [81] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现显式推理不一定提升多模态情感识别（MER）的准确率，但能增强可解释性。快速思维（直接回答）在召回率上优于慢速思维（推理后回答），而慢速思维在精确率上更优。为此提出MER-R1框架，通过双目标解耦和快慢置信度校准，联合优化召回率和精确率，实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理型多模态大模型（MLLMs）在MER任务中，显式推理虽然提升可解释性，但未必带来更高的识别准确率。作者希望探索快慢思维各自的优势，并将其互补性转化为显式优化，使推理真正有益于情感识别。

Method: 提出MER-R1，一种强化学习框架：(1) 双目标解耦，将召回率和精确率分离为两个优化信号，实现联合优化而非折衷；(2) 快慢置信度校准，将慢速思维的回答与快速思维的直觉对齐，增强正确情感、抑制错误情感；(3) 理论证明了该协同机制能缓解优化过程中的方差干扰。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion基准上达到SOTA性能，验证了快慢思维互补性优化能有效提升情感识别效果。

Conclusion: 快慢思维的互补性可以通过显式优化框架实现协同，使推理真正服务于多模态情感识别任务，既保持慢速思维的高精确率，又保留快速思维的高召回率。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [82] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree of Evidence (ToE)，一个层级证据推理框架，通过强化学习驱动的多源检索、证据评估和参数树聚合算法，实现对AI生成虚假新闻的可解释自动化核查，在多个数据集上相比基线提升4-24个百分点，尤其在对抗性毒化输入上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成引擎优化(GEO)毒化使AI生成的虚假新闻能够被检索系统系统性地呈现，污染大语言模型的推理，传统事实核查方法难以应对这种对抗性内容。

Method: 提出ToE框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 强化学习驱动的多源检索智能体；(2) 证据评估智能体；(3) 参数树聚合算法。将每个声明建模为动态扩展的参数树，通过迭代分解、检索和验证构建可解释的证据链。同时从理论上推导了检索过程的形式化误差界，证明学习策略收敛到信息论最优策略的邻域。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干大语言模型上，ToE相比竞争基线实现了4到24个百分点的提升，尤其在对抗性毒化输入上优势更为显著。

Conclusion: ToE通过层级证据推理和强化学习检索策略，有效增强了自动化事实核查在对抗性环境下的鲁棒性和可解释性，为应对AI生成虚假新闻的传播提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出符号反馈驱动的迭代自精炼框架，通过自然语言提示映射逻辑符号、符号验证器识别错误并转化为修正指令、以及计划识别器推断目标可达性，显著提升LLM在长时域规划任务中的鲁棒性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时域决策任务中常产生不可行或错误解，其在可靠性和鲁棒性方面存在严重安全隐患，因此需要有效提升其规划能力。

Method: (1) 自然语言提示机制将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述，增强LLM对任务约束和语义的理解；(2) 符号验证器识别错误并将其转化为LLM可解释的修正指令，引导自精炼；(3) 计划识别器推断目标可达性，提供更有效的目标导向引导。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在长时域规划任务中持续提升了计划的可行性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效增强了基于LLM的规划可靠性，有望推动更可信赖的AI系统发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [84] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了图世界模型（GWM）中的长时域rollout误差，提出统一框架分析动态边与静态边场景下的误差传播机制，并开发了Error-Aware GWM方法，通过谱正则化、rollout一致性和关键节点加权防止长时域发散，在合成拓扑和真实图基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多针对向量或图像等连续空间设计，但许多规划环境本质上是图结构（如智能体、工具、技能、路径和依赖关系图）。在这些图环境中，局部预测误差可能通过图结构传播扩散，且当边是动态预测而非静态固定时，误差传播模式会进一步变化，亟需对图世界模型中的长时域rollout误差进行理论分析和方法改进。

Method: 1) 提出统一框架，涵盖固定边和动态边两种GWM设置，引入动作节点支持节点级、边级和图级决策；2) 推导图值rollout误差界，将拓扑诱导的放大与模型诱导的放大分离；3) 提出联合节点-边算子用于动态边rollout；4) 基于理论分析提出Error-Aware GWM，包含谱正则化、rollout一致性和关键节点加权三个组件。

Result: 在合成拓扑和异构智能体-图测试平台上：rollout误差和规划遗憾随horizon增长而增加；当结构动态演化时需要动态边训练；Error-Aware GWM能防止长时域发散同时保持预测精度。真实图基准测试表明GWM在动态图rollout和智能体规划任务上最有效，而专用图模型在静态或稀疏预测任务上仍占优势。

Conclusion: 图世界模型的长时域rollout误差受拓扑和模型双重因素影响，Error-Aware GWM有效缓解了发散问题。GWM的适用场景在于动态图rollout与智能体规划，而非静态预测任务。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [85] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了基于agent和参数化的世界模型在语言智能体规划任务中的表现，并提出GILP方法，通过结合小型参数化骨干网络与LLM推理，显著降低幻觉状态率并提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种世界模型各有优劣：基于agent的模型灵活但存在不可量化的幻觉错误；参数化模型误差可测但规划能力弱。需要一种方法结合两者优势，同时降低幻觉并提升规划性能。

Method: 提出Grounded Iterative Language Planning (GILP)，仅训练小型参数化骨干网络（提供有效动作、状态增量预测、风险和价值），结合LLM的推理能力（生成动作与想象增量），通过一致性门控机制在两者不一致时要求修订，实现迭代式规划。

Result: 在GPT-4o-mini上，幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035；模拟消融实验中，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的额外LLM调用。

Conclusion: GILP通过参数化模型与LLM推理的协同，有效减少了基于agent的世界模型的幻觉问题，同时以较低的计算成本显著提升了规划成功率，为语言智能体的世界模型设计提供了实用范式。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [86] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATOD提出了一种退火式回合感知的在线蒸馏方法，通过结合OPD和RL的优势，在长程交互任务中既实现快速模仿学习又推动奖励驱动的探索，显著提升了小语言模型智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，在线策略蒸馏（OPD）早期学习快但性能天花板受限于教师模型，而强化学习（RL）能突破上限但早期学习效率低下。作者希望利用两者的互补性，设计一种既能快速模仿又能通过探索超越教师的混合训练算法。

Method: (1) 退火式OPD-RL调度：训练初期以OPD为主，快速接近教师行为；随着训练推进，逐步增强RL比重，驱动奖励驱动的探索。(2) 回合级不一致-不确定性重加权（T-DUR）：在长轨迹中软性放大高价值回合的监督信号，提升密集监督的质量。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个基准上，ATOD在三种学生模型规模下平均成功率达到：超过OPD 3.03个百分点，超过GRPO 23.62个百分点，且超过对应的教师模型2.16个百分点。

Conclusion: ATOD通过退火式结合OPD与RL，并引入回合级重加权机制，有效解决了小语言模型在长程交互任务中模仿学习与探索优化的冲突，实现了超越教师模型的性能。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [87] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NormAct基准，评估多模态大模型在具身导航中隐含社会规范的遵守能力。实验发现模型在显式目标达成（67.3%）与隐含规范遵守（26.4%）之间存在显著差距，并提出NormPerceptor方法将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估主要关注显式目标达成或直接规范知识，缺乏评估模型能否在行动序列中推断并应用隐含社会规范的能力。作者旨在揭示并弥补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建NormAct基准，将隐含规范嵌入日常任务中，从目标达成、规范遵守和总体任务成功三个维度评估。提出NormPerceptor——一种上下文条件化线索生成器，在规划前推断与场景相关的规范。

Result: SOTA MLLM（GPT-5.4等）在显式目标上达成率67.3%，但隐含规范遵守率仅26.4%。线索条件实验表明差距源于规范激活与情境化困难。NormPerceptor将任务成功率从24.2%提升到46.7%。

Conclusion: 具身智能体需要主动检测隐含规范、将其锚定于视觉证据并整合为行动规划约束，这对部署在社会环境中的机器人至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [88] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SD-GPS框架，通过在形式化和推理阶段将符号求解器作为执行预言机，解决了几何问题求解中神经符号方法在自动形式化和定理预测两个核心阶段的瓶颈问题，在Geometry3K和PGPS9K数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号几何求解框架存在两个严重瓶颈：(1) 自动形式化将多模态翻译视为与下游求解器兼容性解耦的静态任务；(2) 定理预测阶段因固定规则库导致求解器频繁陷入演绎困境。

Method: (1) 求解器驱动的自动形式化：基于QwenVL3-2B构建统一模块，融合监督形式语言适应和可解性引导的强化学习，以可执行性为核心训练信号；(2) 验证性定理提出：引入困境感知智能体，从当前证明状态提出局部辅助引理，并通过符号验证过滤所有提议确保可靠性。

Result: 在Geometry3K和PGPS9K数据集上，SD-GPS在标准完成、多项选择和跨模态参考等多个评测设置下，一致优于现有MLLM、纯神经和神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 多模态感知与符号执行之间的闭环能够显著提升几何推理能力，为神经代理通过形式系统实现可验证的问题求解提供了深刻见解。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor. However, current frameworks suffer from severe bottlenecks in two core stages: autoformalization, which treats multimodal translation as a static task decoupled from downstream solver compatibility, and theorem prediction, where solvers frequently hit a deductive impasse due to fixed rule libraries. To address these, we propose SD-GPS, a solver-driven framework that treats the symbolic solver as an execution oracle throughout both formalization and deduction. First, Solver-Driven Autoformalization unifies supervised formal-language adaptation and solvability-guided reinforcement learning into a single module built on QwenVL3-2B, making executability the central training signal. Second, Verified Theorem Proposing introduces an impasse-aware agent that proposes local auxiliary lemmas from current proof states, ensuring soundness by filtering all proposals through symbolic verification. Empirical evaluations on Geometry3K and PGPS9K demonstrate that SD-GPS consistently outperforms existing MLLM, neural, and neuro-symbolic methods across standard completion, multiple-choice, and cross-modal reference regimes, proving that closing the loop between multimodal perception and symbolic execution significantly improves geometric reasoning, offering profound insights into how neural agents can be grounded by formal systems to achieve verifiable problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [89] [RelBall: Relation Ball with Quaternion Rotation for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27967)
*Yike Liu,Peijia Xie,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelBall通过模变换和尾实体关系球扩展了Rotate3D，能够建模层次结构和多种基数关系，在知识图谱补全中覆盖所有关系模式，在多个数据集上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC模型如RotatE和Rotate3D无法同时建模非交换组合、语义层次结构以及一对多等多基数关系，亟需一个统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出RelBall模型，包含两个创新：1) 模变换（modulus transformation）将抽象概念映射到小模量、具体实例映射到大模量以建模层次结构；2) 以尾实体为中心的关系球（tail-centric relation ball）建模一对一、一对多、多对一和多对多关系。

Result: 在多个数据集上的链接预测实验表明RelBall相较于多种基线模型具有竞争性的性能。

Conclusion: RelBall成功覆盖所有关系模式（对称、反对称、逆、交换/非交换组合、层次结构及各种基数关系），且提供了可解释的层次化表示。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge graphs are often incomplete, lacking many valid facts. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to predict missing links using known triples, thereby enhancing graph coverage. A key challenge is modeling diverse relational patterns such as symmetry, antisymmetry, inversion, composition and semantic hierarchy. Existing models such as RotatE can capture symmetric, antisymmetric, inverse, and commutative composition patterns, yet struggle with non-commutative composition. Rotate3D addresses this by introducing non-commutativity via three-dimensional rotations, but still fails to capture the semantic hierarchies prevalent in knowledge graphs. Moreover, both models cannot effectively model one-to-many relations. To overcome these limitations, we propose RelBall, which extends Rotate3D with two innovations. First, our model introduces modulus transformation to model hierarchies, driving abstract concepts toward smaller moduli and concrete instances toward larger ones. Second, it introduces a tail-centric relation ball to model one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. RelBall offers the following advantages: (1) coverage of all relational patterns, including the ones mentioned above; (2) an interpretable hierarchical representation where the modulus directly reflect semantic levels; (3) support for one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate RelBall's competitive link prediction performance against various baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Lifted Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28024)
*Malte Luttermann,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化因果因子图（PCFG）及其部分有向变体（PD-PCFG），并设计提升因果推理（LCI）算法，在关系域中实现 lifted 级别的因果效应计算，显著加速推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 lifted 推理技术应用于因果推断，以解决关系域中因果效应计算的计算效率问题，避免在命题层面进行昂贵的推理。

Method: 引入参数化因果因子图（PCFG）对因果知识进行建模，定义干预的形式语义；提出提升因果推理（LCI）算法在 lifted 层面计算因果效应；进一步提出部分有向 PCFG（PD-PCFG）处理部分因果知识，并扩展 LCI 以适用更广泛的模型。

Result: 与命题级因果推断（如因果贝叶斯网络）相比，LCI 算法显著加速了因果效应的计算；PD-PCFG 扩展了 lifted 因果推断的适用范围，降低了对因果关系先验知识的需求。

Conclusion: Lifted 推理技术可有效应用于关系域中的因果推断，大幅提升计算效率，且通过 PD-PCFG 可处理部分因果知识场景，增强方法的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Lifted inference exploits indistinguishabilities in probabilistic graphical models by using a representative for indistinguishable objects, thereby speeding up query answering while maintaining exact answers. In this article, we show how lifting can be applied to efficiently compute causal effects in relational domains. More specifically, we introduce parametric causal factor graphs (PCFGs) to incorporate causal knowledge in lifted models and give a formal semantics of interventions therein. We further present the Lifted Causal Inference (LCI) algorithm to compute causal effects on a lifted level, thereby drastically speeding up causal inference compared to propositional inference, e.g., in causal Bayesian networks. In addition, we present partially directed parametric causal factor graphs (PD-PCFGs) as a generalisation of PCFGs to handle partial causal knowledge and extend LCI to perform lifted causal inference in a PD-PCFG, thereby extending the applicability of lifted causal inference to a broader range of models requiring less prior knowledge about causal relationships.

</details>


### [91] [JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC) V1: An Industrial-Scale LLM/VLM-Centric Solution for Item Understanding, Management, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28070)
*Oxygen AIIC,Chan Long,Chao Liu,Chaofan Chen,Chaohui Dong,Chunyuan Guo,Danping Liu,Debin Liu,Deping Xiang,Fulai Xu,Guangyue Liu,Hao Li,Huichun Hu,Jian Yang,Jianan Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Jiaoyang Li,Jiaxing Wang,Jinglong Li,Jinjin Guo,Jun Fang,Jun Liu,Kai Zhou,Li Wang,Lili Gao,Liying Chen,Luning Yang,Mengdi Zhou,Pengzhang Liu,Qi Lv,Qianyun Wang,Qixia Jiang,Ruyue Li,Shimu Liang,Shuxing Wang,Sijie Zhang,Siqi Li,Tianhao Gao,Wang Ke,Weihu Huang,Wencan Lai,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaohui Zhang,Xiaojing Dong,Ya Liu,Yifeng Zhang,Yixiang Wang,Yongtai Zhang,Yongyi Liao,Zhaoru Chen,Zhen Chen,Zhiyong Ma,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhongwei Liu,Ziyan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 京东提出了基于大语言模型/视觉语言模型的工业级商品知识生产与服务平台Oxygen AIIC，覆盖数十亿SKU，实现了94.2%精确率和82.8%召回率的知识生产，日均处理数亿次商品更新，搜索流量覆盖率达80.4%，商品信息质量问题下降37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 京东作为拥有超7亿活跃用户、数十亿SKU的超大规模电商平台，面临快速涌现的新概念、海量SKU的高质量知识生产以及多样化的下游需求三大工业级挑战，亟需构建高效、可扩展的商品知识生产与服务体系。

Method: Oxygen AIIC围绕四大核心支柱构建：(i) 高效人机协同驱动的本体工程，支持含数百万条目的本体动态演化；(ii) "语义搜索后判别"(S2D)知识识别架构，结合吞吐量优化策略实现可扩展、高吞吐的AI商品库生产；(iii) 自演化商品理解LLM/VLM，以稳定可控方式持续提升，实现知识生产94.2%精确率和82.8%召回率；(iv) 统一商品通道作为数据与服务枢纽。系统部署于华为昇腾NPU。

Result: Oxygen AIIC已覆盖数万京东类目，日均处理数亿次商品更新，积累数千亿商品知识资产；搜索流量覆盖率80.4%，商品信息质量问题下降37%，商品上架核心属性自动填充率超80%。

Conclusion: Oxygen AIIC作为基于LLM/VLM的工业级商品知识平台，成功解决了超大规模电商场景下的知识生产与服务挑战，在搜索、推荐、运营、品类规划等核心业务场景中取得了可量化的规模化收益。

Abstract: JD.com, one of the world's largest e-commerce platforms, serves over 700 million active users and millions of merchants, with a catalog of tens of billions of SKUs. At this scale, high-quality, structured item knowledge underpins a better consumer experience, lower management costs, and higher operational efficiency-yet producing and serving it poses three industrial-scale challenges: fast-emerging concepts, high-quality knowledge production for massive SKUs, and diverse downstream requirements. To address these challenges, we present the JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC), an industrial-scale platform built on LLMs/VLMs for item-knowledge production and service. Oxygen AIIC is built around four core pillars: (i) ontology engineering driven by efficient human-AI collaboration, which supports the dynamic evolution and agile expansion of an ontology with millions of entries; (ii) a "Semantic Search then Discrimination"(S2D) knowledge identification architecture that, combined with throughput improvement strategies, enables scalable, extensible, and high-throughput AI Item Library production for tens of billions of SKUs; (iii) self-evolving item-understanding LLMs/VLMs that improve in a stable and controllable manner, enabling knowledge production with 94.2% precision and 82.8% recall; and (iv) a unified item tunnel that serves as the data and service hub. Oxygen AIIC now covers tens of thousands of JD categories and processes hundreds of millions of item updates per day on Huawei Ascend NPUs. It has accumulated hundreds of billions of item-knowledge assets. Deployed across core business scenarios-including search, recommendation, operations, category planning-Oxygen AIIC has delivered measurable gains at scale. Search-traffic coverage reaches 80.4%, item-information quality issues drop by 37%, the automated fill rate of core attributes during item listing exceeds 80%.

</details>


### [92] [Ontology-Guided Evidence Path Inference for Multi-hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28076)
*Yongxue Shan,Meihan Wu,Cundi Fang,Jie Peng,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OPI框架，通过引入关系中心的本体图进行双向检索和迭代优化，有效解决多跳KGQA中搜索空间膨胀和语义约束违反的问题，在WebQSP、CWQ和MetaQA上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于主题中心扩展的多跳KGQA方法面临两个关键挑战：搜索空间随噪声混合类型路径快速增长，以及检索路径可能无法满足复杂问题的语义约束。

Method: 提出OPI框架，包含：(1) 构建关系中心本体图以捕获关系的头尾类型约束；(2) 双向检索机制：预测答案类型并映射到兼容的最终跳关系，结合主题侧前缀扩展与答案侧最终跳匹配；(3) 迭代优化策略：在问题上下文中重新评估检索路径和候选答案，过滤类型兼容但问题无关的证据。

Result: 在WebQSP上Hit@1/F1提升4.6/5.0分，在CWQ上提升8.9/3.3分，在MetaQA上仅用检索模块即达到近乎饱和的Hit@1。

Conclusion: OPI通过本体引导的证据路径推理有效抑制了噪声混合类型扩展，缩小搜索空间，提升复杂问题上的答案预测可靠性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) aims to answer natural-language questions by reasoning over structured facts. Existing multi-hop KGQA methods mainly rely on topic-centered expansion, which faces two key challenges: the search space rapidly grows with noisy mixed-type paths, and retrieved paths may fail to satisfy the semantic constraints of complex questions. To address these challenges, we propose OPI, an ontology-guided evidence path inference framework for multi-hop KGQA. OPI introduces a relation-centric ontology graph to capture the head-tail type constraints of relations, providing a compact interface for answer-side constraints. Based on this ontology graph, OPI first introduces a bidirectional retrieval mechanism by mapping the predicted answer type to compatible final-hop relations and combining topic-side prefix expansion with answer-side final-hop matching, thereby suppressing noisy mixed-type expansion. OPI further adopts an iterative refinement strategy to reassess retrieved paths and candidate answers under the question context, filtering type-compatible but question-irrelevant evidence for more reliable answer prediction. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and MetaQA show that OPI substantially reduces the search space, improves Hit@1/F1 by 4.6/5.0 points on WebQSP and 8.9/3.3 points on CWQ over the strongest prior results, and achieves near-saturated Hit@1 on MetaQA with the retrieval module alone.

</details>


### [93] [AI-Driven Synthesis for High-Tech System Design: Automating Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28126)
*Luuk Oerlemans,Steven Westerhof,Theo Hofman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自动化设计（AiD）范式，利用基于深度学习和生成式AI的计算设计综合（CDS）框架，通过两个案例（电驱动系统设计和空间尺寸问题）证明其从仿真优化向自主设计转变的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代高科技系统设计中固有的组合复杂性挑战。

Method: 提出计算设计综合（CDS）框架，利用深度学习和生成式AI实现新型系统的自动化创建。

Result: 两个案例研究（电驱动系统设计和空间尺寸问题）验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: AI驱动方法代表了工程领域的根本性转变，从基于仿真的优化迈向最少人工监督的自主设计。

Abstract: This article addresses the combinatorial complexity inherent in modern high-tech system design by presenting automation-in-design (AiD) as a transformative paradigm. We propose computational design synthesis (CDS), a framework utilising deep learning and generative AI to automate the creation of novel systems. Two case studies (e-drive system design and spatial dimensioning problem) serve as proof-points for this approach. The AI-driven methods used in the case studies represent a fundamental shift in engineering, advancing from simulation-based optimisation towards autonomous design with minimal human supervision.

</details>


### [94] [Tandem Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28166)
*Difan Jiao,Raghav Singhal,Robert West,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL)，将Tandem Training范式扩展到RLVR中，通过让强模型（senior）与冻结的弱模型（junior）交替协同生成推理链并共同获得奖励，在保持推理能力的同时提升了与弱模型及人类的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽大幅提升了大模型的推理能力（如竞赛数学），但其推理模式趋向于低可读性、语言混杂等特征，导致弱模型或人类难以有效利用其推理结果。需要一种方法在保持强模型推理能力的同时，使其推理过程更易于被弱模型或人类理解跟随。

Method: 提出TRL方法：训练更强的senior模型与冻结的较弱junior模型随机交替协同生成推理链，二者作为团队获得联合奖励，仅对senior应用标准GRPO损失。在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上使用竞赛数学数据进行训练。

Result: TRL在单人推理能力上与标准GRPO持平，同时从同一rollout结构中涌现出三个性质：更强的与junior的交接鲁棒性、更少的分布漂移、以及更易于junior理解的思维链。

Conclusion: TRL为RLVR提供了一条有前景的路线，在多模型通信和人类兼容性方面具有实际收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capability of large language models, reaching expert or even superhuman performance in domains such as competition math. However, whether weaker agents and humans can actually harness this capability is far less certain, with RLVR documented to drift reasoning toward idiosyncratic patterns such as poor readability and language mixing. Tandem training is a recently introduced paradigm that targets this compatibility problem: a trained, stronger senior co-generates each rollout with a frozen, weaker junior, and the two are rewarded as a team, so the senior is pushed to reason in ways the junior can follow. Yet this paradigm has so far been demonstrated only in proof-of-concept settings, leaving open whether it scales to the long chains of thought of the modern RLVR pipeline. In this work, we propose Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), which carries the tandem training paradigm into RLVR. In TRL, the senior and a frozen junior alternate stochastically to co-generate the reasoning, the resulting generation is rewarded, and the standard GRPO loss is applied to the senior. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on competition math, we find that TRL matches vanilla GRPO on solo reasoning capability while three properties emerge together from the same rollout structure: stronger handoff robustness with the junior, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and a chain-of-thought more legible to the junior. Our results demonstrate a promising route for RLVR with practical payoffs in multi-model communication and human compatibility.

</details>


### [95] [Agent-Native Immune System: Architecture, Taxonomy, and Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28270)
*Bo Shen,Lifeng Chang,Tianyuan Wei,Yunpeng Li,Feng Shi,Yichen Han,Peijie Gao,Shiyi Kuang,Xin Chang,Dehui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个生物启发的内生式Agent原生免疫系统（ANIS），通过六层免疫塔、Agent病毒/疫苗统一分类学、Harness Triad自监控框架和持续免疫学习（CIL），将防御机制嵌入智能体的认知循环内部，以解决传统外部防御难以防范运行时劫持（如记忆投毒、工具链操纵、多智能体协议攻击）的根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御机制（边界安全、训练时对齐）位于智能体的主动推理循环外部，无法有效防御运行时攻击（记忆投毒、工具链操纵、多智能体协议攻击），即使完全对齐的智能体在面对运行时劫持时依然高度脆弱。

Method: 提出ANIS架构，包含：（1）六层免疫塔（L0-L5），其中L1为屏障免疫层（非认知的物理/逻辑隔离）；（2）Agent病毒与Agent疫苗的统一分类学，区分浅层非参数化防御与稳健参数化疫苗；（3）Harness Triad（Meta、Self、Auto）自监控元认知自动化框架，驱动持续免疫学习（CIL）实现疫苗动态适应新威胁；（4）严格区分模型对齐（静态"宪法"价值基础）与Agent免疫（动态"执法"机制）。

Result: 构建了首个内生于智能体认知循环的生物启发式防御架构，提出了Agent病毒/疫苗的形式化分类框架，建立了持续免疫学习机制，并明确了模型对齐与Agent免疫之间的理论分界。

Conclusion: ANIS为Agent安全开辟了新范式，但面临免疫协议标准化、Autoimmunity Rate（假阳性干预率）等新评估指标、以及病原体与疫苗在集体智能生态系统中的协同演化动力学等开放性挑战。

Abstract: The transition from static chat bots to autonomous agents--equipped with persistent memory, tool-use protocols, and multi-agent collaboration--has fundamentally expanded the AI threat landscape. Current defense mechanisms, such as perimeter security and training-time alignment, remain external to the agent's active reasoning loop. Consequently, they fall short: a fully aligned agent remains highly vulnerable to runtime hijacking via memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, or multi-agent protocol attacks. To address this critical gap, we introduce the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), the first biologically inspired, endogenous defense architecture embedded directly within the agent's cognitive loop. Our framework presents four primary contributions. First, we design a six-layer Immune Tower (L0-L5), distinctly incorporating Barrier Immunity (L1) as a non-cognitive, physical-and-logical isolation layer. Second, we establish a unified taxonomy of Agent Viruses and Agent Vaccines, formalizing the critical distinction between superficial non-parametric defenses and robust parametric vaccines. Third, we conceptualize the Harness Triad--Meta, Self, and Auto--a self-monitoring, meta-cognitive automation backbone that drives Continual Immune Learning (CIL), enabling vaccines to dynamically adapt to novel threats. Finally, we establish a rigorous theoretical demarcation between model alignment and agent immunity: while alignment provides a static "constitutional" value foundation during training, ANIS serves as the dynamic "law enforcement" mechanism during runtime. We conclude by framing open challenges for the field, including immune protocol standardization, novel evaluation metrics such as the Autoimmunity Rate (false-positive intervention rate), and the co-evolutionary dynamics between pathogens and vaccines within collective intelligence ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Test-Input Generation for Tensor Programs: What Actually Finds Kernel Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27396)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了张量核测试输入的七种生成策略，发现边界形状采样在26个算子（含16个正确和10个LLM风格错误）上达到78%召回率和0%假阳性，而对抗性值采样虽召回率99%但假阳性高达94%。边界形状对softmax尾部掩码错误提升显著（从0%到100%/62%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量核的测试输入生成长期依赖经验法则（选一个代表性形状和dtype跑allclose检查即发布），缺乏系统性的选择显式化和度量研究。

Method: 基于gpuemu操作语义感知的种子化模糊测试器，在RTX 3060 GPU上对26个算子（16个正确控制+10个基于文档化转录模式注入的LLM风格错误）评估七种策略，策略在形状候选集、dtype组合和输入值分布三个维度变化，从错误召回率和控制假阳性率两个轴报告结果。

Result: 边界形状采样：78%召回率，0%假阳性；对抗性值采样：99%召回率但94%假阳性（因注入NaN/Inf导致验证器的NaN检查对所有传播NaN的核都触发）；对两个softmax尾部掩码错误，常规策略召回率0%，边界形状分别提升至100%和62%。

Conclusion: 边界形状采样是操作上安全的最优策略；不同策略对不同注入错误模式有选择性，结果反映的是策略对种子化错误模式的捕获能力，而非任何特定部署LLM的错误率。

Abstract: Test-input generation for tensor kernels is folkloric. Most projects pick a representative shape and dtype, run a fixed-shape allclose-style check, and ship. We make the choices explicit and measure them. Using the gpuemu op-schema-aware seeded fuzzer (arXiv:2606.20128), we evaluate seven test-generation strategies across a 26-op corpus (16 correct controls and 10 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription patterns) on an RTX 3060 GPU instance. Strategies vary the shape candidate set, the dtype mix, and the input value distribution. We report each strategy on two axes: bug recall and control false-positive (FP) rate. Boundary-only shape sampling is the operationally safe winner: 78% recall on the 10 buggy kernels with 0% FP on the 16 controls. Adversarial value sampling reaches higher recall (99%) but inflates control FP to 94% because the strategy injects NaN and Inf inputs and the validator's NaN check fires on every kernel that propagates them, not only on buggy kernels. On the two softmax tail-mask bugs the "regular" strategy (no boundary shapes) catches 0%, while boundary raises recall to 100% and 62% respectively. That gap is the clearest single signal in the data. The corpus result is about which seeded bug patterns each strategy catches, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM.

</details>


### [97] [Implementing GenAI-Supported Learning in Software Engineering and Computer Science Education using Bloom's Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27398)
*Vahid Garousi,Zafar Jafarov,Aytan Mövsümova,Leyla Memmedova,Hüseyn Mirzayev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于布鲁姆分类法的生成式AI使用框架，通过在多个软件工程/计算机科学课程中嵌入教学指导，发现学生认为GenAI对高阶认知活动（分析、评价、反思）最有价值，且明确的层级指导能促使学生反思性地使用AI，甚至在有意识的情况下选择延迟或不用AI。研究表明，GenAI的教育价值在于认知目标、教学指导与学习者自我调节的有意对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程教育中的采用带来了学习支持的机会，同时也引发了关于浅层学习和学术诚信的担忧。本研究旨在探索如何通过基于布鲁姆分类法的明确教学指导，支持学生在SE/CS教育中负责任地使用GenAI，并考察学生和教师对GenAI辅助学习的看法。

Method: 设计了一个布鲁姆对齐的GenAI框架，明确不同认知层级下GenAI的适当角色。该框架被嵌入贝尔法斯特女王大学和阿塞拜疆技术大学多个SE/CS课程的课程说明、实验和评估中。通过匿名问卷和学习工件收集数据，使用主题分析法并以布鲁姆分类法作为分析视角进行分析。

Result: 学生认为GenAI对高阶认知活动（分析、评价、反思）最有价值，对基础学习不太适用。明确的布鲁姆层级指导促使学生反思性地使用GenAI，在需要独立思考时会有意识地延迟使用或选择不使用。学生和教师均报告了教学益处，同时也指出了认知负担和教学设计工作量方面的挑战。

Conclusion: GenAI的教育价值在于认知学习目标、教学指导和学习者自我调节之间的有意对齐。布鲁姆分类法为SE/CS教育中负责任地使用GenAI提供了一个可扩展的、以教学法驱动的框架，为以执行为中心的应对方案提供了实用替代方案。

Abstract: Context: Generative AI adoption in software engineering education raises opportunities for learning support alongside concerns about superficial learning and academic integrity.
  Objective: This study investigates how explicit instructional guidance aligned with Bloom's taxonomy supports responsible GenAI use in SE/CS education, exploring student and instructor perceptions of GenAI-supported learning.
  Method: We designed a Bloom-aligned GenAI framework that articulated appropriate GenAI roles at different cognitive levels. The framework was embedded in course instructions, labs, and assessment across multiple SE/CS courses at Queen's University Belfast and Azerbaijan Technical University. Data were collected via anonymous questionnaires and learning artifacts, analyzed using thematic analysis with Bloom's taxonomy as an analytic lens.
  Results: Students perceived GenAI as most valuable for higher-order cognitive activities (analysis, evaluation, reflection) and less suitable for foundational learning. Explicit Bloom-level guidance influenced students to use GenAI reflectively, with delayed or intentional non-use when independent thinking was prioritized. Both students and instructors reported pedagogical benefits alongside challenges in cognitive effort and instructional design workload.
  Conclusion: GenAI's educational value lies in intentional alignment between cognitive learning goals, instructional guidance, and learner self-regulation. Bloom's taxonomy provides a scalable, pedagogy-driven framework for responsible GenAI use in SE/CS education, offering a practical alternative to enforcement-focused responses.

</details>


### [98] [Formal Grammars in Business Process Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27399)
*Milliam Maxime Zekeng Ndadji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了34篇关于形式文法与业务流程管理交叉领域的研究，识别出七大研究流，揭示了形式文法在BPM各生命周期阶段的应用，但各流派并行发展，缺乏跨流综合。提出了五个基于语料库的开放挑战，呼吁更深层次、统一地利用文法理论推动BPM发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式文法作为一种生成性、声明性和分析性兼备的形式化工具，在业务流程管理中展现出独特优势，但相关研究分散于多个方向，缺乏系统性梳理和跨流派综合。本综述旨在整合现有知识，识别开放挑战，为未来研究提供统一视角。

Method: 对34篇在形式文法与BPM交叉领域的主要研究进行系统性文献综述，通过归纳分析识别出七大研究流，对每个流派的贡献、所用形式化方法和局限性进行综合。

Result: 识别出七大研究流：流程文法、流程建模语言的本体论评估、生产规则文法、属性文法、图文法、文法推断、以及进程代数。形式文法已影响BPM从组织设计到形式验证和数据驱动发现的全生命周期，但七大流派基本并行发展。

Conclusion: 对形式文法理论进行更深入、统一的利用，有望显著推动BPM领域的发展。论文指出了五个基于语料库的开放挑战，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Business Process Management (BPM) is concerned with the systematic design, execution, monitoring, and improvement of business processes. Formal grammars have emerged as a particularly fruitful formalism for BPM, offering generative, declarative, and analytical capabilities that are uniquely well-suited to process-oriented concerns. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 34 primary studies at the intersection of formal grammars and BPM. We identify seven research streams: (i) process grammars for organizational process design; (ii) process modeling languages evaluated as grammars under the Bunge-Wand-Weber ontological framework; (iii) production-rule grammars for process structural specification and variant management; (iv) attribute grammars for the declarative specification and distributed execution of workflows; (v) graph grammars for the transformation, generation, and semantic analysis of process models; (vi) grammatical inference for process mining and discovery; and (vii) process algebras as grammar-like compositional frameworks for behavioral specification and verification. For each stream, we synthesize contributions, formalisms employed, and limitations. The review reveals that formal grammars have influenced BPM across every lifecycle phase (from organizational design to formal verification and data-driven discovery) yet the seven streams have developed largely in parallel, without cross-stream synthesis. We identify five corpus-grounded open challenges and argue that a deeper, unified exploitation of grammatical theory holds significant promise for advancing the state of the art in BPM.

</details>


### [99] [Recall Before Rerank: Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Large-Scale Code-to-Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27401)
*Leonardo Venuta,Francesco Tosoni,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统性评估了深度学习模型在大规模代码-代码搜索中第一阶段召回的有效性、效率和可扩展性，提出了基于LLM的代码标准化与查询重写方案，并指出了当前模型在精度与可扩展性上的关键局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义代码搜索和克隆检测对软件开发、维护和复用至关重要，而当前深度学习模型在TB级源代码集合上的精度与可扩展性存在关键局限，需要系统评估和改进。

Method: 跨多种编程语言和数据集对当代深度学习模型进行基准测试，提出基于LLM的代码标准化与查询重写方案。

Result: 基于LLM的代码标准化和查询重写方案在低性能模型上取得了显著的精度提升；揭示了当前代码专用LLM在跨数据集上假设的鲁棒性存在问题。

Conclusion: 对资源受限部署的可持续性提出了质疑，并为构建可扩展、高效的代码检索系统提供了可行见解。

Abstract: Semantic code search and clone detection are essential for software development, maintenance, and reuse. This paper evaluates the effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability of contemporary deep learning models for first-stage recall in large-scale code-to-code search engines. Benchmarking across multiple programming languages and datasets reveals critical limits in the precision and scalability of these models on Terabyte-scale source-code collections. We present LLM-based code normalisation and query-rewriting schemes that yield significant gains in precision for lower-performing models. Our results question the sustainability of resource-constrained deployment and the assumed robustness of current code-specialised LLMs across datasets. We conclude with actionable insights for building scalable, efficient code-retrieval systems.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Evaluation of Implicit Software World Models in Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27406)
*Egor Bogomolov,Yaroslav Zharov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出软件世界模型（Software World Model）概念，将评估维度从传统的控制流（测试结果、异常类）扩展至执行资源（峰值内存、墙钟时间、方法级和行级性能分析输出）。基于SWE-bench Verified数据集测试发现，包括前沿模型在内的所有模型表现均不理想，说明当前AI对软件执行的理解远不如对源代码编写的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码执行基准仅覆盖控制流这一狭窄维度，缺乏对执行资源（如内存、时间、性能分析）的系统评估，导致无法全面衡量模型对软件行为（软件世界模型）的理解能力。

Method: 以SWE-bench Verified为数据源，将评估指标从测试结果和异常类扩展至峰值内存、墙钟时间、方法级和行级性能分析排序输出；在多个模型（包括前沿模型）上进行实验测试。

Result: 所有被测模型表现中等且行为脆弱（brittle），表明模型对软件如何执行的理解显著不足，与其对源代码编写的能力形成对比。

Conclusion: 当前AI在对软件执行过程的理解上存在明显短板，需要更全面的评估框架来推动软件世界模型的构建与改进。

Abstract: Software engineering, whether performed by humans or by AI agents, requires reasoning about how software behaves. We call the internal model that supports such reasoning the software world model, and view current code-execution benchmarks as covering one well-studied slice of it -- control flow. In this paper, we take a step toward a broader evaluation by shifting the observable axis to execution resources: alongside test outcome and exception class, we predict peak memory, wall-clock time, and ranked profiler outputs at method and line granularity. We use SWE-bench Verified as the source of data to hold the test close to real-world software engineering tasks. All tested models, frontier ones included, show modest performance and brittle behaviour, suggesting a notable lack of understanding of how software is executed, as opposed to how its source code is written.

</details>


### [101] [Speculative Refinement: A Hybrid Autoregressive Diffusion Decoding Strategy and Its Behavior Across Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27474)
*Aditi Gupta,Neel Mishra,Kushagra Trivedi,Pawan Kumar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过SpecRef混合解码方法，揭示了当前评估体系在生成模型（尤其是结合自回归与扩散模型）时存在的四个关键问题：代码基准混淆了结构与逻辑正确性、多阶段修正存在"精炼张力"现象、似然评估与生成评估结果不一致、以及标准后处理对非自回归模型不兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法无法有效衡量结合自回归和扩散解码的混合生成系统，需要更诊断性的评估实践。

Method: 提出SpecRef方法（基于熵引导选择性掩码的扩散模型精炼），在六个基准上用三种评估协议进行系统分析。

Result: 发现四个关键问题：代码基准中语法脚手架可提升20%+准确率；多阶段修正会破坏已正确token；似然评估与生成评估产生不同排序；Python后处理对非自回归生成器失效。

Conclusion: 这些发现适用于任何多阶段或非自回归生成流水线，指向更具诊断性的评估实践。

Abstract: How should we evaluate generation systems that combine autoregressive (AR) and diffusion decoding? We study this question through Speculative Refinement (SpecRef), a training-free hybrid method that warm-starts a masked diffusion language model from an AR draft using entropy-guided selective masking. Evaluating SpecRef across six benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, GSM8K, BBH, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag) with three distinct evaluation protocols (execution-based pass@1, exact-match, log-likelihood scoring), we surface several findings relevant beyond our specific system: (1) code benchmarks conflate structural discovery with logical correctness: providing a syntactic scaffold lifts accuracy from near zero to over 20% without changing the model, indicating that much of the baseline failure is structural; (2) a refinement tension phenomenon where multi-stage correction degrades already-correct tokens, exposing benchmark saturation ceilings invisible to single-model evaluation; (3) log-likelihood and generative evaluation produce different model rankings for the same model pair, suggesting they measure different capabilities; (4) standard Python post-processing silently breaks code evaluation for non-AR generators. These observations apply to any multi-stage or non-autoregressive generation pipeline and point toward more diagnostic evaluation practices.

</details>


### [102] [Contextual Associations Between Webpage Elements for Web Accessibility: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27506)
*Kishan Rakesh,Shiyi Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过链接预测从无障碍树中恢复网页元素间的上下文关联，以帮助屏幕阅读器用户理解孤立元素（如"Read more"）。作者构建了人工标注数据集，比较了四种机器学习模型与两种启发式基线，并在35个网站上进行跨站点泛化分析。初步实验显示Hit@10在0.16-0.85之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕阅读器用户在按元素列表导航网页时，常遇到符合W3C规范但在上下文中无法理解的名称（如"Read more"）。周围有意义的元素存在于页面中，但没有任何机制将其与目标元素关联起来，尚无工作实证研究如何选择与目标相关的上下文元素。

Method: 在Tranco前百万列表中的35个网站上构建人工标注的上下文关联数据集（每页三位独立标注者），将页面从无障碍树转换为图结构，并增强DOM和CSS的空间与语义特征。比较MLP、GCN、GAT、SEAL四种模型与两种启发式基线，采用留一网站交叉验证，使用Hit@K和MRR指标。

Result: 预注册统计框架下的五站点作者标注试点实验显示：四种学习模型的Hit@10范围为0.16-0.85，两种启发式基线范围为0.08-0.30。最终结果将在完整实验和分析完成后报告。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了网页上下文关联的人工标注数据集、基于无障碍树图的链接预测用于上下文选择的实证评估，以及跨站点泛化分析。

Abstract: [Context] Screen reader users navigating webpages by element list often encounter accessible names such as "Read more" that are valid under the W3C Accessible Name and Description Computation specification but uninterpretable in isolation. The surrounding elements that would make these names meaningful exist in the page but are not linked to the target by any mechanism. No prior work has empirically studied how to select which surrounding elements are contextually relevant to a given target. [Objective] This registered report investigates whether human-perceived contextual associations between webpage elements can be recovered from the accessibility tree using link prediction, and whether the learned associations generalize across websites. [Method] We will construct a dataset of human-annotated contextual associations on 35 websites, stratified across the Tranco top-million list, with three independent annotators per page. Each page is represented as a graph derived from its accessibility tree, augmented with spatial and semantic features from the DOM and CSS. We compare four machine learning models (MLP, GCN, GAT, and SEAL) against two heuristic baselines under leave-one-site-out cross-validation with a pre-registered statistical framework, using Hit@K and MRR. [Results] We have conducted a five-site author-annotated pilot study to establish the pipelines and parameterize the power simulation, with pilot Hit@10 ranging from 0.16 to 0.85 across four learned models and 0.08 to 0.30 across two heuristic baselines. The final results will be reported after the planned experiments and analyses are completed. [Conclusion] The study contributes a human-annotated dataset of contextual associations on webpages, an empirical evaluation of link prediction for context selection on accessibility-tree graphs, and a cross-site generalization analysis.

</details>


### [103] [Test Case Selection for Deep Neural Networks: A Replication Study on LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27601)
*Ali Asgari,Mitchell Olsthoorn,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对深度神经网络测试用例选择（TCS）技术在大型语言模型代码模型上的有效性进行了大规模复制研究，发现只有部分基于视觉DNN的结论得以泛化：基于不确定性的特征在早期故障发现上有效，基于表示的特征在精度估计上更鲁棒，且TCS效果高度依赖任务和模型上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TCS技术实证评估几乎完全集中在基于视觉的DNN和数据集上，不清楚这些发现是否能泛化到LLM代码模型领域。因此需要在代码分类任务上系统验证TCS技术的可复制性。

Method: 在三个代码分类任务（克隆检测、漏洞检测、技术债务预测）上，对17个微调后的任务特定模型实例、7个预测特征、13种选择策略（含12种特征感知策略和简单随机采样基线）进行了大规模复制研究，从精度估计和早期故障发现两个维度评估性能。

Result: 只有部分视觉DNN的TCS发现能泛化到LLM代码模型：不确定性特征在早期故障发现上有效，表示特征在精度估计上更鲁棒；但效果在不同任务和模型间差异显著，表明TCS有效性具有上下文依赖性。

Conclusion: 本研究为TCS技术超越视觉深度学习的可复制性提供了实证证据，并为LLM代码模型的操作化评估提供了使用见解。

Abstract: Recently, test case selection (TCS) techniques have been explored to support the operational evaluation of deep neural networks (DNNs) under limited testing budgets, where labeling cost is a primary concern and uncovering model failures early is a key objective. Although prior studies report promising results, existing empirical evaluations focus almost exclusively on vision-based DNNs and datasets, leaving it unclear whether prior findings generalize to LLM code models. This paper presents a large-scale replication study of TCS techniques in the context of LLM code models. We re-examine established TCS strategies originally proposed for DNNs and complement them with statistical sampling strategies not previously evaluated for TCS. We assess their effectiveness on three code-related classification tasks: clone detection, vulnerability detection, and technical debt prediction. The study spans 17 task-specific fine-tuned model instances, 7 predictive features, and 13 selection strategies, including 12 feature-aware strategies and simple random sampling (SRS) as a feature-agnostic baseline. We evaluate performance along two dimensions: accuracy estimation and early failure discovery. The results indicate that only a subset of findings reported for vision-based DNNs generalize when TCS is applied to LLMs for code. In particular, uncertainty-based features are effective for early failure discovery, while representation-based features are more robust for accuracy estimation. At the same time, performance varies substantially across tasks and models, indicating that TCS effectiveness is context-dependent. Overall, this study provides empirical evidence on the replicability of TCS techniques beyond vision-based deep learning and offers insights into their use for the operational evaluation of LLMs for code.

</details>


### [104] [LLM-Assisted Model-Based GUI Testing for Vue.js Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27665)
*Tao Li,Chenhui Cui,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLMVue框架，利用大语言模型（GPT-4o）从Vue.js源代码自动生成页面转换图（PTG），实现模型驱动的GUI自动化测试。在10个开源Vue.js项目上的评估表明，生成的PTG具有高精度和召回率，且基于LLMVue的测试在覆盖率和探索效率上显著优于随机探索基线。这是首次将LLM用于Vue.js应用的模型驱动GUI测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vue.js作为流行的现代Web框架，其可靠性的自动化测试至关重要。虽然模型驱动测试已在其他平台成功用于GUI测试自动化，但应用于Vue.js面临挑战：转换候选（分布在路由配置和单文件组件SFC中）需要被具体化并标准化为可执行的页面转换图（PTG）以进行测试。现有方法难以处理Vue.js组件层次结构和路由转换的复杂分布。

Method: 提出LLMVue框架，使用大语言模型（GPT-4o）从Vue.js源代码生成PTG。具体地，LLM推断组件层次结构和路由转换，将跨多个SFC的信息合并为统一的PTG。框架包括对路由配置和SFC的分析，通过LLM进行候选转换的具体化和标准化。

Result: 在10个GitHub开源Vue.js项目上评估，使用GPT-4o作为LLM骨干。构建的图显示出高精度和召回率，且图编辑距离低。与随机探索基线相比（相同时间约束下），LLMVue引导的测试显著提升了覆盖率和探索效率。

Conclusion: 这是首次将LLM用于Vue.js应用的模型驱动GUI测试，通过源级PTG提取实现自动化测试。LLMVue有效解决了Vue.js中转换候选分散的挑战，验证了LLM在GUI模型构建中的潜力。

Abstract: Vue.js is a popular framework for building modern web applications. As Vue.js functionality and tooling support grow, ensuring its reliability (through automated testing) is becoming increasingly important. Although model-based testing has been successfully used to automate graphical user interface (GUI) testing on other platforms, its application to Vue.js remains challenging: Transition candidates, which are spread across router configurations and single-file components (SFCs), must be concretized and normalized into an executable page transition graph (PTG) for testing. To address this, we propose the LLMVue framework, which uses a large language model (LLM) to generate a PTG from Vue.js source code. LLMVue infers component hierarchies and route transitions, merging them into a unified PTG across multiple SFCs. We evaluated LLMVue on a collection of ten open-source Vue.js projects from GitHub, using GPT-4o as the LLM backbone. The constructed graphs demonstrate high precision and recall, with low graph edit distance. LLMVue -guided testing also significantly improves the coverage and exploration efficiency, compared to a random exploration baseline (with the same time constraints). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first use of LLMs for model-based GUI testing of Vue.js applications using source-level PTG extraction.

</details>


### [105] [BashCoder-R1: Towards Robust and Explainable Bash Code Generation with Robustness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27733)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jia Xu,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jiajia Ma,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Zenghua Wang,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BashCoder-R1是一个面向Bash脚本生成的框架，通过持续预训练、长链思维监督微调和鲁棒性感知强化学习，在BashBench基准上显著超越了DeepSeek-V3.2等强基线模型，在语法正确性、鲁棒性和功能完整性上均达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的Bash脚本存在两大问题：不可审计的"黑盒"推理过程，以及生成代码中关键鲁棒性漏洞。这两个问题相互关联，亟需一个既能提供可解释推理又能保证代码鲁棒性的解决方案。

Method: 提出BashCoder-R1框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 持续预训练(CPT)使模型专门化Bash范式；(2) 长链思维监督微调(L-CoT SFT)在专家验证的推理-代码样本上训练，模拟主动风险感知思维；(3) 鲁棒性感知组相对策略优化(R-GRPO)，通过加权奖励优化语法正确性、鲁棒性（基于shellcheck）和格式正确性。

Result: 在BashBench基准（952个真实任务）上，单行/多行任务分别达到：SyntaxPass 100.00%/94.97%，RobustWarnRate 4.01%/16.47%，RobustPass 95.99%/79.33%，FuncRate 93.01%/93.85%，FullRate 90.04%/73.18%。FullRate上分别超越最强基线DeepSeek-V3.2达37.82%和20.18%。

Conclusion: BashCoder-R1在功能性、鲁棒性和清晰度的人工评估中均获得最高质量评级，证明该框架能有效同时解决Bash脚本生成中的可解释性缺失和鲁棒性不足两大问题。

Abstract: Bash scripts are the cornerstone of system administration and DevOps automation, where code quality directly impacts system stability and security. In automated Bash script generation using Large Language Models (LLMs), two interconnected failures emerge: unauditable "black box" reasoning and critical robustness vulnerabilities in generated code. To address both, we propose BashCoder-R1, a novel framework for robust and explainable Bash script generation. Our pipeline combines: (1) Continual Pre-training (CPT) to specialize the model on Bash paradigms; (2) Long Chain-of-Thought Supervised Fine-Tuning (L-CoT SFT) on expert-validated reasoning-and-code samples to emulate proactive risk-aware thinking; and (3) Robustness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (R-GRPO), a reinforcement learning phase optimizing a weighted reward for syntax correctness, robustness (via shellcheck), and format correctness. We evaluate on BashBench, a new benchmark of 952 real-world tasks (773 single-line, 179 multi-line). BashCoder-R1 achieves SyntaxPass (100.00%/94.97%), RobustWarnRate (4.01%/16.47%), RobustPass (95.99%/79.33%), FuncRate (93.01%/93.85%), and FullRate (90.04%/73.18%) for single-line/multi-line tasks, outperforming the strongest baseline DeepSeek-V3.2 (Reasoning) by 37.82% and 20.18% in FullRate. Human evaluation on Functionality, Robustness, and Clarity further confirms BashCoder-R1 achieves the highest quality ratings.

</details>


### [106] [UNICS: Multilingual Code Search via Unified Pseudocode and Contrastive Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27747)
*Ye Fan,Jidong Ge,Chuanyi Li,Liguo Huang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UNICS是一个两阶段训练的多语言代码搜索框架，首阶段使用伪代码作为统一表示进行跨语言算法级逻辑预训练，第二阶段通过多任务迁移学习（包括语义切片分解、困难正样本挖掘和跨语言动态困难负样本采样）适配特定语言，在多语言和跨语言基准上达到最先进性能，尤其在低资源语言的零样本迁移中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练模型在多语言代码搜索中面临三个主要障碍：数据不平衡、跨语言语义干扰，以及现有统一表示（如AST或IR）丢失关键信息。此外，传统对比学习策略通常依赖简单的困难负样本采样，忽视了挖掘困难正样本来学习代码内在语义不变性的潜力。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段使用伪代码作为统一表示，在自建数据集上预训练以学习保持完整语义保真度的跨语言算法级逻辑；第二阶段采用多任务迁移学习，将代码分解为语义切片（如API调用、函数体），并引入困难正样本挖掘和跨语言动态困难负样本采样任务。

Result: 在多个多语言和跨语言代码搜索基准上达到最先进的性能，展现出优越的泛化能力和性能平衡，尤其在低资源语言的零样本迁移任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: UNICS通过伪代码统一表示保留完整语义、语义切片分解减少跨语言干扰、以及困难正/负样本挖掘策略，有效解决了多语言代码搜索中的关键挑战，证明了该框架在不同语言场景下的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: While pre-trained models have achieved remarkable success in code search, their multilingual capabilities remain a major hurdle, plagued by data imbalance, cross-lingual semantic interference, and the loss of critical information from existing unified representations like Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) or Intermediate Representations (IRs). Furthermore, conventional contrastive learning strategies often rely on simplistic hard negative sampling while overlooking the potential of mining hard positives to learn code's intrinsic semantic invariance. To address these challenges, we introduce UNICS, a framework for multilingual code search built on a two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, UNICS is pre-trained on a novel dataset we constructed, which uses pseudo-code as a unified representation to learn a cross-lingual, algorithm-level logic that preserves full semantic fidelity. The second stage employs a multi-task transfer learning strategy that adapts this general knowledge to specific languages by decomposing code into semantic slices (e.g., API calls, function bodies) and incorporating tasks for hard positive mining and cross-lingual dynamic hard negative sampling. Experimental results demonstrate that UNICS achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple multilingual and cross-lingual benchmarks, showcasing superior generalization and performance balance, especially in zero-shot transfer tasks to low-resource languages.

</details>


### [107] [Reasoning Beyond Prediction: From Data-Driven to Causal Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27960)
*Roberto Pietrantuono,Luca Giamattei,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文呼吁一种新的人机协作范式：机器不仅自动化常规任务或从学习模式中预测，还应通过因果推理主动增强软件工程师的推理能力，以应对日益复杂的软件工程挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程的复杂性持续增长（涵盖AI驱动产品、分布式云原生架构、嵌入式网络物理环境），现有的基于深度学习的协同工程方法和工具虽然增强了一定程度的自动化和决策支持，但仍远不能满足现代软件开发所需的智能支持，因此需要新的范式。

Method: 提出一种以因果推理为核心的人机协作新范式，让机器能够主动增强工程师的推理能力，而不仅仅是自动化或基于模式预测。

Result: 指出当前深度学习方法在提供智能支持方面的不足，论证了引入因果推理范式的必要性。

Conclusion: 随着软件本身变得更加智能，需要更智能的支持方式，即从"自动化"和"预测"转向"因果推理增强"，以实现真正的人机协作。

Abstract: Software engineering is an intellectually demanding, creative discipline that juggles a web of interdependent tasks to design, build, and assure the quality of increasingly complex systems. As our expectations from software soar - with demands spanning AI-driven products, pervasively distributed and cloud-native architectures, and deeply embedded cyber-physical environments - its complexity steadily increases. In response, a new wave of co-engineering methods and tools, fueled by deep learning, has emerged to augment the process, enhancing automation and decision support. Yet, these advances remain far from delivering the kind of intelligent support that modern software development demands. We call for a new paradigm of human-machine cooperation: one where machines don't just automate routine tasks or predict from learned patterns, but actively amplify engineers' reasoning through the lens of causation. As software becomes smarter, a smarter support is needed.

</details>


### [108] [Evolution-Aware Regression Test Prioritization of ML-Enabled Systems Using Gradient-Based Behavior Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28037)
*Eunho Cho,Donghwan Shin,In-Young Ko*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GBV-PD方法，利用梯度行为向量空间实现ML系统进化感知的回归测试优先级排序，无需在排序时运行进化模型，在分类与回归任务中优于非定向基线，并支持缓存复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML组件的持续演化（重训练、微调、优化）会使原有测试结果失效，需要高效的回归测试优先级排序方法，且该方法必须能够感知演化的方向性（即性能变化有增有减）。

Method: 将每个测试用例表示为基于梯度的行为向量（GBV），即其在原始模型下损失梯度的低维PCA投影；然后将进化模型的参数更新投影到同一PCA基上，通过对齐度估算各测试用例损失增减趋势，无需在排序时运行进化模型。

Result: 在分类和回归任务上，GBV-PD一致优于非定向基线方法，与全梯度参考方法竞争力相当，且通过GBV缓存实现在多次更新下更优的时间和存储开销。

Conclusion: 行为空间的思想可以被实际落地为一种高效实用的机制，用于持续演化的ML系统的回归测试。

Abstract: The machine learning(ML) component of an ML-enabled system evolves through retraining, fine-tuning, and optimization, so previously valid test results may no longer hold. A single evolution step can worsen performance on some test cases while improving others, making regression test prioritization inherently directional. We present Gradient-based Behavior Vector-Parameter Delta(GBV-PD), the first approach to operationalize the behavior vector space for evolution-aware regression test prioritization. GBV-PD represents each test case as a gradient-based vector(GBV), a low-dimensional projection of its loss gradient under the original model. It then projects the observed parameter update of the evolved model onto the same PCA basis and uses the resulting alignment to estimate whether each test case's loss is likely to increase or decrease, without running the evolved model on test cases during prioritization. In an empirical study across classification and regression tasks, GBV-PD consistently outperformed non-directional baselines and remained competitive with a full-gradient reference, while offering better time and storage profiles for repeated updates via reusable GBV caching. These results show that behavior-space ideas can be operationalized into a practical and efficient mechanism for repeated-update regression testing of evolving ML-enabled systems.

</details>


### [109] [SBridge: Identifying Source-to-Binary Function Similarity via Cross-Domain Control Block Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28058)
*Heedong Yang,Jeongwoo Lee,Hajin Yun,Seunghoon Woo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SBridge是一种基于控制块匹配的跨源-二进制函数相似性检测方法，能在约40%函数被内联的条件下，实现75.13% recall@1和80.98% recall@5，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全领域中检测二进制中复用的已知代码（如漏洞传播）至关重要。虽然二进制间比较可行，但以源码为参考更实用（易获取、无需编译）。然而源码与二进制之间存在显著鸿沟（如函数内联、剥离符号），现有方法依赖字符串字面量或整体结构相似性，无法捕获细粒度行为，导致大量误报。

Method: SBridge提出**控制块匹配**的创新方法：将函数划分为有意义的单元（如条件块、循环块）作为跨域表示，封装核心功能特征，从而精确度量源码与二进制之间的函数相似性，有效应对函数内联和剥离二进制带来的挑战。

Result: 在BinKit收集的3,904个真实C/C++二进制上，尽管约40%的二进制函数被内联，SBridge仍取得75.13% recall@1和80.98% recall@5，而现有方法最高仅达到43.31% recall@1和50.2% recall@5。

Conclusion: SBridge通过控制块作为跨域表示，有效弥合了源码与二进制之间的语义鸿沟，在函数内联场景下仍能实现高精度的相似函数识别，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We present SBridge, a precise approach for identifying functions in binaries that are similar to the given source code functions. Identifying reused code in binaries is critical for security, particularly for detecting propagated vulnerabilities. Although binary-to-binary comparison is feasible, leveraging source code as the reference is more practical because source code is easier to collect and analyze directly without compilation. However, significant gaps between source and binary representations, including function inlining, create challenges in cross-domain function detection. Existing approaches primarily rely on string literals or structural similarities between entire functions, failing to capture detailed code behavior and generating many false alarms. SBridge addresses these limitations through a key innovation: control block-based function matching, which encapsulates essential functional features by segmenting functions into meaningful units such as conditionals and loops. Leveraging control blocks as a cross-domain representation, SBridge enables precise measurement of function similarity between source and binary code, effectively overcoming challenges posed by function inlining and stripped binaries. For evaluation, we collected 3,904 real-world C/C++ binaries from BinKit. In experiments identifying binary functions identical to input source functions, despite approximately 40% of binary functions being inlined, SBridge achieved 75.13% recall@1 and 80.98% recall@5, outperforming existing approaches, which achieved up to 43.31% recall@1 and 50.2% recall@

</details>


### [110] [The ARDoCo Tool Landscape: REST API, TraceView, and TraceViz for Architecture Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28064)
*Jan Keim,Dominik Fuchß,Sophie Corallo,Tobias Hey,Julian Winter,Kevin Feichtinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ARDoCo工具生态，包含REST API、TraceView前端和TraceViz VS Code扩展三个组件，通过HTTP端点提供四种溯源链接恢复管线，并在IDE中可视化链接，初步研究证实其能提升开发者理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构文档、架构模型与源代码之间的链接关系对维护和一致性检查至关重要，但自动化溯源链接恢复在实践中部署困难。

Method: ARDoCo REST API提供四种TLR管线（SAD-SAM、SAM-Code、SAD-Code、SAD-SAM-Code），支持异步执行和缓存；TraceView提供基于浏览器的引导式向导和多面板交互探索；TraceViz是VS Code扩展，在IDE中直接叠加显示溯源链接。

Result: 三个组件均已公开部署并可实际使用。TraceViz的初步研究证实其内IDE可视化在软件理解任务中能提升开发者的理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工具生态使最新TLR技术对架构师、开发者和工具集成者更加可用。

Abstract: Context and Problem. Software development produces interrelated artifacts like software architecture documentation (SAD), software architecture models (SAMs), and source code, whose relationships are essential for maintenance and consistency checking. However, automatically recovering links between these artifacts (traceability link recovery (TLR)) remains difficult to deploy in practice. Method and Aim. We present an accessible tool landscape for ARDoCo's TLR approaches: the ARDoCo REST API exposes four TLR pipelines (SAD-SAM, SAM-Code, SAD-Code, and SAD-SAM-Code) via HTTP endpoints with asynchronous execution and caching; TraceView is a browser-based frontend with a guided wizard and interactive multi-panel exploration of recovered links and inconsistencies; and TraceViz, which is a VS Code extension that overlays trace links directly onto documentation in the IDE. Results and Conclusion. All three components are publicly deployed and usable. A preliminary study for TraceViz's in-IDE visualization confirmed that it improves developer comprehension during software understanding tasks. The tool landscape makes state-of-the-art TLR accessible to architects, developers, and tool integrators. Video. We provide a screencast of our ARDoCo Tool Landscape and how it is used here: https://youtu.be/IOTEPZQ3tVs

</details>


### [111] [How Humans, Bots, and Agents Communicate About Vulnerabilities in Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28125)
*Pien Rooijendijk,Christoph Treude,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究计划分析人类、机器人和编码代理在Pull Request讨论中如何通过显式标识符（如CVE/GHSA）和隐式安全语言（如"未授权访问"）交流漏洞信息，并探讨这些引用与代码漏洞引入/修复以及审查活动之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作主要关注显式漏洞标识符，可能遗漏了没有正式引用的漏洞讨论。同时，机器人和编码代理在PR中日益普及，需要探究不同账号类型如何交流漏洞信息。

Method: 基于AIDev-pop数据集，分析PR标题、描述、审查评论、提交消息和时间线讨论中的显式漏洞引用和隐式安全信号，并研究这些引用与代码修改中引入或修复的漏洞、PR审查活动和结果的关联。

Result: 这是一篇注册报告（registered report），描述的是计划中的研究，尚未提供具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究将贡献一项关于现代软件开发中漏洞沟通实践的大规模实证调查。
