Abstract: Developers may reference vulnerabilities in pull request discussions through both explicit identifiers, such as CVEs or GHSAs, and implicit security-related language (e.g., "unauthorized access" or "SQL injection"). Prior work has primarily focused on explicit identifiers, potentially overlooking vulnerability discussions that lack formal references. Bots and coding agents are becoming more common in pull requests, raising new questions about how different accounts communicate about vulnerabilities. In this registered report, we describe our planned study of vulnerability communication in pull requests by humans, bots, and coding agents. Building on the AIDev-pop dataset, we analyze explicit vulnerability references and implicit security-related signals across pull request titles, descriptions, review comments, commit messages, and timeline discussions. We further investigate whether these references are associated with vulnerabilities introduced or fixed in the modified code and how they relate to pull request review activity and outcomes. This study contributes a large-scale empirical investigation of vulnerability communication practices in modern software development.

</details>


### [112] [CrossLangFuzzer: Differential Testing of Cross-Language JVM Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28132)
*Xiaotian Ma,Qiong Feng,Yongqiang Tian,Wei Song,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出首个跨语言JVM编译器的差异测试框架CrossLangFuzzer，利用Kotlin编译器统一中间表示合成跨语言测试程序，并通过七种变异算子增强测试多样性，在五个主流JVM编译器上发现32个已确认的Bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代JVM软件日益集成多种编程语言（Java、Kotlin、Groovy、Scala等），跨语言编译时需协调不同语言的语义差异，可能导致严重误编译错误；现有编译器测试技术仅关注单语言编译，缺乏跨语言场景的测试方法。

Method: 利用Kotlin编译器的统一中间表示(IR)合成跨语言测试程序，设计并应用七种变异算子（mutation operators）对生成的测试程序进行多样化处理，构建差异测试（differential testing）框架。

Result: 在五个主流JVM编译器最新版本上测试，共发现32个已确认Bug，其中Kotlin 15个、Groovy 4个、Scala 3 7个、Scala 2 2个、Java 4个。

Conclusion: CrossLangFuzzer能有效发现跨语言JVM编译中的缺陷，填补了跨语言编译器测试领域的空白，工具已开源。

Abstract: Modern JVM software increasingly integrates multiple programming languages, such as Java, Kotlin, Groovy, and Scala, within a single application. Supporting such interoperability requires JVM compilers to perform cross-language compilation while reconciling subtle semantic differences across language boundaries. Errors in this process can lead to critical miscompilations, yet existing compiler testing techniques focus exclusively on isolated, singlelanguage compilation. To address this gap, we present CrossLangFuzzer, the first differential testing framework for cross-language JVM compilation. CrossLangFuzzer leverages the Kotlin compiler's unified intermediate representation (IR) to synthesize cross-language test programs. It further applies seven mutation operators to diversify generated test programs and improve bug-finding capability. Evaluated on the latest versions of five major JVM compilers, CrossLangFuzzer uncovered 32 confirmed bugs, including 15 in Kotlin, 4 in Groovy, 7 in Scala 3, 2 in Scala 2, and 4 in Java. CrossLangFuzzer is open-source at https://github.com/XYZboom/CrossLangFuzzer

</details>


### [113] [Humanizing Automatically Generated Unit Test Suites with LLM-Based Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28229)
*Wendkûuni C. Ouédraogo,Yinghua Li,Xueqi Dang,Paweł Borsukiewicz,Lingfeng Bao,Anil Koyuncu,Jacques Klein,David Lo,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestHumanizer 是一种混合 SBST+LLM 方法，将 LLM 作为受控重构层应用于 EvoSuite 生成的可编译测试套件，通过改进命名、结构和可读性来提升测试的开发者友好度，同时保留行为和编译有效性。在 350 个类上的评估表明，该方法达到 88-98% 的编译率，结构覆盖率仅下降 1-2 个百分点，并显著提升了可读性、降低了复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的测试生成工具（如 EvoSuite）能产生可编译且高覆盖率的测试套件，但可读性和可维护性差；LLM 能生成更自然的测试，但直接生成的编译率仅为 51-78%。现有方法无法兼得高编译率与高可读性。

Method: 提出 TestHumanizer 混合方法：以 EvoSuite 生成的可编译测试套件为基础，使用 LLM（gpt-4o 和 mistral-large-2407）作为受控重构层，在三种上下文配置（含摘要设置）下对测试进行命名、结构和清晰度改进，并通过验证门控确保行为保留和编译有效性。

Result: 在 350 个类（Defects4J 和 SF110）上评估，EvoSuite 每类生成 15 个套件，经重构共产生 31,500 个测试套件。编译率达 88-98%，接近 EvoSuite 的 100%；结构覆盖率通常仅波动 1-2 个百分点；86-95% 的重构满足忠实重构阈值；可读性提升，控制流和认知复杂度降低，结构坏味减少。开发者研究（30 类、444 个测试方法）确认感知可读性和采用意愿显著提升（Wilcoxon p<0.01，评分者间一致性高）。

Conclusion: LLM 最有效的角色不是独立生成器，而是作为基于 SBST 稳健输出的、经过验证门控的细化层。基于摘要的上下文设置提供了最稳健的权衡，而长代码中心提示更易产生幻觉导致的失败。

Abstract: Search-based test generation tools such as EvoSuite produce compilable and high-coverage unit tests at scale, but their suites are often hard to read and maintain. LLMs can generate more natural tests, yet direct generation remains brittle, with compilation rates of only 51-78% in our study. We introduce TestHumanizer, a hybrid SBST+LLM approach that uses LLMs as controlled refactoring layers over compilable SBST suites to improve naming, structure, and developer-oriented clarity while preserving behavior and compilation validity. We evaluate TestHumanizer on 350 classes from Defects4J and SF110. EvoSuite generates 15 suites per class, and each suite is refactored under three context configurations using gpt-4o and mistral-large-2407, yielding 31,500 refactorings. TestHumanizer reaches 88-98% compilation rates, close to EvoSuite's 100% baseline and clearly above direct LLM generation. Structural coverage is largely preserved, typically within 1-2 percentage points, and 86-95% of refactorings satisfy a composite faithful-refactoring threshold. Refactored suites also improve predicted readability, reduce control-flow and cognitive complexity, and mitigate structural smells. The summary-based setting offers the most robust trade-off, while long code-centric prompts are more prone to hallucination-induced failures. A developer study on 30 classes and 444 test methods confirms significant gains in perceived readability and willingness to adopt, with Wilcoxon p less than 0.01 and substantial inter-rater agreement. Overall, LLMs are most effective not as standalone generators but as validation-gated refinement layers over robust SBST outputs.

</details>


### [114] [Govern the Repository, Not the Agent: Measuring Ecosystem-Level Risk in AI-Native Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28235)
*Daniel Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究发现，在超过93万个AI智能体提交的PR中，约一半的集成摩擦（整合贡献的成本）归因于仓库本身而非单个智能体。AI智能体贡献造成的仓库级摩擦集中度是人类的两倍（组内相关系数0.30 vs 0.16），表明风险是生态系统属性而非智能体属性，AI原生软件应在生态系统层面而非单智能体层面进行度量和治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系逐个评测AI编码智能体在孤立基准任务上的表现，但各智能体通过各自测试后，仓库仍会积累问题。作者质疑这种问题属于个体智能体还是其积累所在的仓库，旨在揭示集成摩擦的来源归属。

Method: 对超过93万个智能体生成的PR进行量化分析，测量集成摩擦的变异中有多少可归因于仓库层面（而非贡献本身、作者、规模或智能体），并对比AI智能体与人类在相同仓库中的摩擦集中度差异，同时控制代码库规模、年龄、任务形态、流程成熟度和合并路径等变量。

Result: 约一半的集成摩擦变异归因于仓库层面，且在全控制变量下仍显著。AI智能体贡献的仓库级摩擦集中度约为人类的两倍（组内相关系数0.30 vs 0.16），该差距在控制多种变量后依然成立。

Conclusion: 集成摩擦风险是生态系统属性而非个体智能体属性，AI原生软件应在生态系统层面（而非逐个智能体）进行度量和治理。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open and merge pull requests in shared repositories at scale, and the field evaluates them the way it has always evaluated components, one agent at a time, on isolated benchmark tasks. Yet agents that each pass their own tests still leave repositories that accumulate problems no single contribution accounts for. We ask whether this problem belongs to the individual agent or to the repository where it accumulates. We study integration friction, the cost of integrating a contribution into a codebase that other contributors are concurrently changing. Across more than 930,000 agent-authored pull requests, we measure how much of the variation in friction stays with the repository after the contribution, its author, its size, and its agent are accounted for. About half does, and it survives full controls. In the same repositories, agent-authored contributions concentrate this repository-level friction roughly twice as much as human ones (intraclass correlation 0.30 versus 0.16), a gap that holds after controlling for codebase size, age, task shape, process maturity, and merge path. The risk is a property of the ecosystem, not the agent. AI-native software is therefore better measured and governed at the ecosystem level than one agent at a time.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [A Sensitivity-Aware Test Collection for Search Among Personal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27559)
*Jack McKechnie,Graham McDonald,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于Enron邮件语料库的敏感性感知搜索（SAS）测试集，包含150个查询公式、11,471个查询-文档相关性评估及敏感性标注，并提供了基线性能结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索系统在返回有效结果时可能泄露敏感个人信息，但缺乏同时包含敏感与非敏感信息、查询及相关性评估的标准测试集来开发敏感性感知搜索模型。

Method: 基于人工标注敏感性的Enron邮件子集，通过众包方式获取50个主题的150个查询公式和11,471个查询-文档相关性评估；利用大语言模型（LLM）按信息检索评估最佳实践扩展评估和敏感性标签；提供稀疏和稠密索引。

Result: 构建了可用的SAS测试集，提供了相关性、敏感性分类和敏感性感知搜索的基线性能结果，并将数据集整合到ir_datasets包及Huggingface平台。

Conclusion: 该数据集为敏感性感知搜索研究提供了标准化评估资源，有助于推动在保护隐私前提下实现有效检索的研究方向。

Abstract: Traditional search tasks aim to satisfy user information needs by returning a subset of a collection of documents, ranked by the documents' relevance to a user query. However, some collections that contain useful information also contain sensitive personal information. Recently, there has been increasing interest in the development of Sensitivity-Aware Search (SAS) retrieval models to provide users with effective retrieval results without revealing such sensitive information. To develop such systems, test collections containing both sensitive and non-sensitive information, a set of queries, and query-document relevance assessments are required. The Enron email corpus contains real business-related emails, where some emails also contain sensitive personal information. However, the original Enron collection does not contain queries or query-relevance assessments. To this end, we crowdsource 150 query formulations for 50 different topics and 11,471 query-relevance assessments for a subset of the Enron documents that have been manually labelled for sensitivity. We follow best practices for using large language models (LLMs) in Information Retrieval evaluation to extend the collection further with additional LLM judged query-relevance assessments and sensitivity labels. We present baseline performances for relevance, sensitivity classification, and sensitivity-aware search on the collection. We make the collection available, including through the popular ir_datasets package, and provide pre-built sparse and dense indices on Huggingface to facilitate easy experimentation.

</details>


### [116] [Intuition-Guided Latent Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27684)
*Chang Liu,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Qifan Wang,Fuli Feng,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出IntuRec框架，通过从用户历史中提取推荐直觉作为潜在先验，引导LLM的潜在推理起点和轨迹，从而提升推荐系统中的偏好推理性能，在多个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐中的潜在推理方法通常从无约束的推理起点出发，导致隐藏表示与目标物品嵌入不对齐，产生次优推理轨迹。受认知神经科学中人类多步推理由直觉引导的启发，作者希望为LLM推荐提供一种偏好对齐的直觉锚点来引导推理。

Method: 提出两阶段框架IntuRec：(1) 提取阶段，LLM推荐器基于用户历史生成top-K候选集作为直觉来源；(2) 注入阶段，利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制将候选集转化为偏好对齐的直觉嵌入，初始化推理起点并引导后续潜在推理。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，IntuRec一致性地优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过提供语义上有根据的推理起点，IntuRec能够沿着更准确的推理轨迹高效探索偏好空间，为LLM推荐中的潜在推理提供了一种有效的范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning capabilities in complex problem-solving tasks, motivating their use for preference reasoning in recommender systems. Latent reasoning, which operates in continuous hidden spaces rather than discrete tokens, has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for LLM-based recommendation. However, existing methods often start from unconstrained reasoning points, where hidden representations are misaligned with target item embeddings, leading to suboptimal reasoning trajectories.
  Inspired by cognitive neuroscience, which suggests that human multi-step reasoning is guided by intuition as a latent prior, we propose \emph{IntuRec}, a two-stage framework that anchors latent reasoning with \emph{recommendation intuition}. In the extraction stage, the LLM-based recommender generates a top-$K$ candidate set based on users' histories as the source of intuition. In the injection stage, the candidate set is transformed into a preference-aligned intuition embedding using self- and cross-attention mechanisms, which initializes the reasoning start point and guides subsequent latent reasoning. By providing a semantically grounded starting point, IntuRec efficiently explores the preference space along more accurate reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that IntuRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. We release our code at https://github.com/Ten-Mao/IntuRec.

</details>


### [117] [Bifocal Diffusion Language Models: Asymmetric Bidirectional Context for Parallel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27732)
*Yuhang Chen,Xianfeng Wu,Jinhao Duan,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Bifocal dLLMs范式，通过非对称双向上下文解决离散扩散语言模型在双向注意力（高质量但无法KV缓存）和因果注意力（可缓存但质量下降）之间的权衡。具体实例化R2LM模型，结合因果注意力和轻量反向Mamba侧边车，在Qwen3-1.7B上续训60B tokens后，批量推理吞吐量比双向dLLM提升2.4-12.9倍，比AR基线提升1.9-2.9倍，质量超越因果基线且平均优于双向dLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散语言模型面临架构设计困境：双向注意力虽质量高但无法使用KV缓存，限制了批处理吞吐量；因果注意力虽支持缓存但损失右侧上下文导致生成质量下降。需要一种既能保持高质量又能高效缓存的解决方案。

Method: 提出Bifocal dLLMs非对称双向上下文范式，具体实例化为R2LM模型，包含两个互补机制：(a) 标准因果注意力提供精确的左上下文并完全兼容KV缓存；(b) 轻量反向Mamba SSM侧边车提供压缩的右侧上下文而不破坏缓存能力。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上续训60B tokens后，R2LM在批处理场景下吞吐量比双向dLLM高2.4-12.9倍，比AR基线快1.9-2.9倍；在大多数基准测试上超越因果基线，平均性能超过双向dLLM。

Conclusion: 非对称双向上下文范式能够有效解决离散扩散语言模型在生成质量和推理效率之间的根本矛盾，R2LM作为该范式的实例化验证了其在实际部署中的优越性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) recover masked tokens in parallel, offering significant speedups over autoregressive (AR) generation. However, such promising frameworks face a fundamental architectural design dilemma: \ding{182} Adopting bidirectional attention achieves strong generation quality by allowing each position to access the full context, but is inherently incompatible with KV caching, limiting inference throughput in batch-serving scenarios; \ding{183} Conversely, causal attention enables efficient cached inference but loses all right-side context, substantially degrading generation quality. This paper introduces Bifocal dLLMs, a new paradigm that resolves this dilemma through \emph{asymmetric bidirectional context}. Analogous to bifocal lenses, we instantiate the paradigm as \textbf{R2LM} (Right-to-Left Mamba), which combines two complementary mechanisms: $a$) standard causal attention providing precise left-context with full KV cache compatibility, while $b$) a lightweight reverse Mamba SSM sidecar supplying compressed right-side context without breaking cacheability. Comprehensive experiments on continued pretraining of Qwen3-1.7B with 60B tokens demonstrate that R2LM achieves $2.4\times$ to $12.9\times$ higher throughput than bidirectional dLLMs and $1.9\times$ to $2.9\times$ speedup over AR baselines in batch serving through parallel decoding with KV caching, while exceeding the causal baseline on most benchmarks and surpassing the bidirectional dLLM on average.

</details>


### [118] [End-to-End Dynamic Sparsity for Resource-Adaptive LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27743)
*Yuhang Chen,Jinhao Duan,Ruichen Zhang,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Tianlong Chen,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出L2A框架，通过预算条件化的轻量门控网络，让LLM在推理时根据动态资源预算（层跳过、头剪枝、推理令牌缩减）自适应调整计算图，单模型即可覆盖完整计算-精度帕累托前沿，在34%层稀疏度下性能损失仅0.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理在静态资源假设下部署，但真实云基础设施资源动态变化（如竞价实例抢占、分层QoS），静态模型在资源约束下会崩溃或浪费计算，缺乏能感知运行时资源预算的自适应推理方案。

Method: 提出L2A框架，将推理建模为受约束的资源分配问题，条件同时依赖于输入和运行时资源预算。引入轻量级、预算条件化且输入感知的门控网络集成到LLM中，通过统一目标联合优化任务性能、逻辑一致性和资源成本。沿三个维度实现自适应：层跳过（内存与深度压力）、头剪枝（吞吐竞争）和推理令牌缩减（延迟收紧）。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-3-4B上，单L2A模型追踪完整计算-精度帕累托前沿。在34%层稀疏度下，GSM8K上性能损失仅0.6%，零样本OOD任务保持相同差距；而静态/启发式基线需要单独调参，且在可比推理时间下性能下降5-10%。

Conclusion: L2A框架证明LLM可以学习预算感知的推理策略，超越仅依赖输入难度的条件，根据实时资源动态自适应配置计算足迹，为动态云环境下的高效LLM部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) inference is typically deployed under a static resource assumption, where models execute a fixed computational graph regardless of the runtime environment. However, real-world cloud infrastructure is inherently dynamic, characterized by fluctuating availability (e.g., spot instance preemption) and tiered Quality-of-Service requirements. In such volatile settings, static models are inflexible: they either crash under resource constraints or waste compute on redundant operations. To bridge this gap, we propose Learning to Allocate (L2A), an end-to-end framework for resource-adaptive inference. Unlike prior methods that condition only on input difficulty, we formulate inference as a constrained allocation problem conditioned on both the input and the runtime resource budget itself. We introduce lightweight, budget-conditioned and input-aware gating networks integrated into the LLM. These gates are trained via a unified objective that jointly optimizes task performance, logical consistency, and resource costs along three axes matching how real-world dynamics manifest: layer skipping for memory and depth pressure, head pruning for throughput contention, and reasoning-token reduction for latency tightening. This lets the model learn a budget-aware policy beyond input difficulty alone: it adaptively configures its computational footprint with respect to real-time resource dynamics, maximizing reasoning depth when resources permit while enforcing strict frugality when budgets tighten. A single L2A model traces the entire compute-accuracy Pareto frontier on Llama-3-8B and Qwen-3-4B: at up to 34% realized layer sparsity, it stays within 0.6% of the dense baseline on GSM8K, with the same gap holding zero-shot on out-of-distribution tasks, while every static or heuristic baseline requires a separately tuned model and still drops by 5-10% at comparable inference time.

</details>


### [119] [From Bootstrapping to Sequence Modeling: A Unified Generative Framework for Personalized Landing-Page Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27865)
*Fan Li,Chang Meng,Jiaqi Fu,Shuchang Liu,Tianke Zhang,Xueliang Wang,Xiaoqiang Feng,Yongqi Liu,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多页面应用中个性化落地页建模（PLPM）问题，本文提出GLAN框架，采用基于决策Transformer的序列建模方法替代传统CQL方法，通过L-RTG模块捕捉用户跨日消费动态提供全局指导，以及HRM模块将会话级反馈分解为细粒度信号提供局部监督，在快手平台线上实验取得DAU和用户生命周期分别提升0.158%和0.108%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CQL的强化学习方法存在两个根本性局限：(1)马尔可夫假设无法捕捉真实用户行为中的强非马尔可夫时序依赖；(2)时序差分学习中的自举法在延迟奖励场景下产生严重累积误差和信用分配困难，特别是在用户每天多次进入应用的长周期场景中。

Method: 提出GLAN框架，采用决策Transformer进行序列建模，从全局-局部统一视角解决PLPM问题。包含两个关键模块：(1)L-RTG模块：捕获用户跨日消费动态，为当日所有页面分配提供精准全局指导；(2)HRM模块：将会话级反馈分解为细粒度信号，为每次页面分配提供精确局部监督。

Result: 在快手平台线上实验中，GLAN相比基线在日活跃用户（DAU）上提升0.158%，在用户生命周期（LT）上提升0.108%。

Conclusion: GLAN通过全局-局部统一的序列建模框架有效解决了CQL方法的非马尔可夫依赖和信用分配问题，在真实大规模推荐系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Modern online platforms increasingly adopt multi-page architectures to accommodate diverse user needs. On these platforms, page navigation (the process of directing users to specific functional pages upon app entry) serves as a critical gateway that shapes user's first impression and significantly influences subsequent engagement. To optimize this process, Kuaishou formulated the task of Personalized Landing Page Modeling (PLPM) and proposed KLAN, a reinforcement learning framework built upon Conservative Q-Learning (CQL). However, CQL-based approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: (1) the Markov assumption fails to capture the strong non-Markovian temporal dependencies inherent in real-world user behaviors, and (2) TD learning with bootstrapping incurs severe cumulative errors and credit assignment difficulties under delayed rewards, particularly in long-horizon settings where users enter the app multiple times daily. To address these limitations, we propose GLAN (Generative Landing-page Adaptive Navigator), a sequence modeling framework built on Decision Transformer to tackle PLPM from a unified global-local perspective. Specifically, GLAN incorporates two key modules. First, we design the L-RTG module that captures users' inter-day consumption dynamics to provide accurate global guidance for all page assignments within a day. Furthermore, we propose the HRM module that decomposes session-level feedback into fine-grained signals, enabling precise local supervision for each page assignment. Extensive online experiments conducted on the Kuaishou platform demonstrate the effectiveness of GLAN, achieving +0.158\% and +0.108\% improvements on Daily Active Users (DAU) and user Lifetime (LT) respectively.

</details>


### [120] [An LLM-Powered Semantic Alignment Framework for Journal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27930)
*Yanglin Yan,Zicheng Xie,Tianchen Gao,Rui Pan,Hansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义对齐框架，将期刊推荐建模为稿件内容与期刊范围描述之间的语义匹配问题。使用DeepSeek-V3在23,609篇文章（49种统计及相关领域期刊）上进行实验，Top-3/5/10准确率分别为40.23%、53.67%和70.05%，无需训练即可实现可解释的期刊推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有期刊推荐方法依赖监督学习、人工特征工程或历史交互数据，泛化能力和可解释性有限，亟需一种无需训练、可推广且可解释的推荐范式。

Method: 将期刊推荐形式化为语义匹配问题，利用LLM直接根据文章标题、摘要、关键词及候选期刊信息推断期刊适合度，无需特定任务训练。采用DeepSeek-V3在统计及相关领域49种期刊的23,609篇文章上进行实验。

Result: Top-3、Top-5、Top-10准确率分别达40.23%、53.67%和70.05%；加入参考文献信息可进一步提升性能；多次运行的平均Top-5 Jaccard相似度为84%，表明结果高度稳定；模型能生成可解释的推理输出。

Conclusion: LLM作为无需训练、可扩展的范式在期刊推荐和学术决策支持方面具有巨大潜力，兼顾性能、稳定性和可解释性。

Abstract: Journal recommendation is an important task in scholarly information systems. Existing approaches typically rely on supervised learning models, manually engineered features, or historical interaction data, which may limit their generalizability and interpretability. We propose an LLM-powered semantic alignment framework that formulates journal recommendation as a semantic matching problem between manuscript content and journal scope descriptions. The framework enables large language models (LLMs) to infer journal suitability directly from article titles, abstracts, keywords, and candidate journal information without task-specific training. Experiments are conducted using DeepSeek-V3 on a dataset of 23,609 articles from 49 journals in statistics and related fields. The proposed framework achieves Top-3, Top-5, and Top-10 accuracies of 40.23\%, 53.67\%, and 70.05\%, respectively. Additional analyses show that incorporating reference information generally improves recommendation performance and that recommendations remain highly stable across repeated runs, with an average Top-5 Jaccard similarity of 84\%. The framework also generates interpretable reasoning outputs that provide insights into the recommendation process. These findings demonstrate the potential of LLMs as a training-free and scalable paradigm for journal recommendation and scholarly decision support.

</details>


### [121] [Listwise Explanation of Embedding-Based Rankings via Semantic Chunk Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27980)
*Hyunkyu Kim,Yeeun Yoo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对密集排序模型中特征单元与表示粒度不匹配的问题，提出ChunkGroupSHAP方法，通过语义分组建模跨文档特征，实现列表级Shapley归因，并发现最佳特征单元取决于排序器类型和检索语料结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有列表级可解释方法仍将排序归因于孤立词语，但密集排序器使用句子和段落级语义表示，导致词语级特征过于碎片化，无法匹配密集语义排序的粒度。

Method: 提出ChunkGroupSHAP，一种列表级Shapley方法，将语义相关的文本块聚类为共享的跨文档特征，掩码一组特征会扰动所有包含相关证据的文档，从而在更接近密集表示的粒度上进行归因，同时保持列表级设定。

Result: 在MS MARCO、FinanceBench、AILACaseDocs和FinQA数据集上，使用E5排序器和BM25进行实验，发现最佳解释单元具有依赖性：词汇级特征适用于BM25，语料级分组适用于密集排序器，查询局部分组适用于异构网页检索。

Conclusion: 特征单元的选择应同时遵循排序器的表示粒度和检索语料的结构特点，而非采用单一固定粒度。

Abstract: Dense embedding rankers score documents through contextual sentence- and passage-level representations. Yet many listwise explanation methods still attribute rankings to isolated words. This feature-unit mismatch leaves word-level features too fragmented for dense semantic ranking. We introduce ChunkGroupSHAP, a listwise Shapley method that clusters semantically related chunks into shared cross-document features. Masking a group perturbs all documents with related evidence, attributing rankings at a granularity closer to dense representations while preserving the listwise setup. Our findings across MS MARCO, FinanceBench, AILACaseDocs, and FinQA with E5 rankers and BM25 show that the best explanation unit is setting-dependent: word features for lexical BM25, corpus-level groups for dense rankers, and query-local grouping for heterogeneous web retrieval. Feature units should thus follow both the ranker's representational granularity and the structure of the retrieved corpus.

</details>


### [122] [Fast and Feasible: Permutation-based Constrained Reranking for Revenue Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28059)
*Svetlana Shirokovskikh,Anastasiia Soboleva,Ekaterina Solodneva,Aleksandr Katrutsa,Roman Loginov,Egor Samosvat*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于排列的轻量级重排序近似算法PermR，用于在电商搜索和推荐系统中最大化付费推广产品收入，同时保证相关性等约束条件。该算法通过相邻交换操作近似求解整数线性规划问题，在离线测试中实现了ILP约63%的收入提升，在包含5600万搜索查询的14天在线A/B测试中收入增加了2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索和推荐系统已能产生高度相关的结果，下一步自然发展是在重排序阶段最大化平台付费推广产品的收入，但单纯最大化收入可能降低用户体验（相关性下降、欺诈风险增加），因此需在约束条件下优化收入。

Method: 将重排序问题建模为带约束的整数线性规划（ILP），目标是最大化收入，约束条件包括相关性等指标；提出轻量级排列近似算法PermR，每步选择相邻项目对进行交换，以改进目标或修复违反的约束。

Result: PermR在离线多类别测试中达到ILP收入改进的约63%，满足生产延迟限制且保持所有约束；在5600万查询的14天在线A/B测试中收入提升2%。

Conclusion: PermR是一种实用、高效的在线重排序算法，能在严格延迟约束下近似最优ILP解，有效平衡商业收入与用户体验指标。

Abstract: Search and recommender systems have produced highly relevant search results. A natural next step in the development of such systems in e-commerce is to rerank these results to increase the platform's revenue from paid promotion products. However, maximizing revenue alone may degrade the user experience by reducing relevance or increasing fraud risk. To avoid this, we state the reranking problem as an integer linear program ($ILP$) that maximizes revenue subject to per-query constraints on other metrics, e.g., relevance. Since solving $ILP$ exactly for every query is slow for deployment to the online service, we propose a lightweight permutation-based reranking approximation algorithm PermR. At each step, the algorithm selects a pair of neighboring items and swaps them to either improve the objective or repair a violated constraint. We evaluate PermR across multiple categories of a large classified platform in offline and online settings. PermR achieves about 63\% of the ILP revenue improvement, within production latency limits, preserving all constraints. In a 14-day online A/B test over 56 million search queries, PermR increased revenue by $2$\%.

</details>
